# Supply network description: Widget

This document describes the "Widget supply network". The network has 7 echelons and 1000 nodes: 84 tier-2 suppliers, 84 tier-1 suppliers, 84 component plants, 84 assembly plants, 84 central distribution centres, 83 regional distribution centres and 497 customers. Planning uses periods of 1 day over a horizon of 90 periods; durations are rounded to the nearest period. The network handles 1 product: Widget (unit value 68).

The vendor is configured with a maximum throughput of 2710 units per day, a fixed charge of 650 per period, a variable cost amounting to 2.76 per unit and a likelihood of failure of 0.02.
